There is a clear signal to society that the Polish constitution and its principles stigmatize and condemn all activities related to the promotion of totalitarian attitudes and prohibit them. This is why in the added chapter 2 the Senator Chamber proposes to include provisions prohibiting the construction of monuments glorifying totalitarianism. The amendment contained in Chapter 1 extends the current provisions to the prohibition of the granting by local government units of names promoting the totalitarian system and extends this provision to the auxiliary units of municipalities which I mentioned in the introduction. For the subject matter of the law, the most important is the regulation concerning the names of settlements, but also in this case it is not possible to exclude the need to apply the law to other subsidiary units of the municipality. The proposed amendment, due to a short period of time for the task carried out by the Institute of National Remembrance - the Commission for the Investigation of Crimes against the Polish Nationals, extends the current deadline in order to carry out studies on the admissibility of the opinion. The draft provides that judicial and administrative proceedings caused by the change of addresses will be exempt from fees and that documents containing the current name will remain valid. Another amendment is to waive the requirement to obtain the permission of the owners of the property on which the internal road is situated to change its name. In addition, Chapter 1 proposes the repeal of the provisions concerning the Council for the Protection of the Remembrance of Combat and Martyrdom, which results from the liquidation of that body. The project provides for a ban on the construction of monuments promoting the totalitarian system. The concept of monuments includes mounds, obelisks, columns, sculptures, statues, busts, memorial stones, inscriptions and signs. A procedure is also introduced to allow voivodships to interfere with the removal of monuments promoting the totalitarian system. In this respect, they will be able to issue decisions ordering the removal of forbidden monuments. A new 12-month period for owners or users of perpetual land to restore the lawful condition of the obligation to remove the monument to which the subject-matter of the Act is indicated was also indicated. The Senate proposes to establish a 3-month period of vacathio legis, which will enable all addressees to prepare for new regulations. The provisions of the project do not fall within the scope of European Union law. High Seym! As I have repeatedly raised from this tribune, the Polish People's Party, which is attached to the values and traditions of independence, national, to the rich history of its members' martyrdom and activists, is in favour of the adoption of the Senate project and in principle does not object to its material part. Thank you for your attention.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u314	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present a draft law amending the Law on Road Traffic and some other laws together with the draft implementing act. The project was prepared by the Ministry of Infrastructure and Construction, is an official government project and found in print no. 1543. High Izbo! The amendment to the Road Traffic Law Act, prepared by the Ministry of Infrastructure and Construction, introduces a new form of temporary registration of vehicles, called professional registration of vehicles. It will enable manufacturers, vehicle distributors and test units to carry out tests without having to register each vehicle at the office. The regulations included in the draft will simplify the activities of companies by relieving them from some of the administrative responsibilities and at the same time they will be able to ensure the legitimate use of vehicles in road traffic. New solutions respond to the demands of the automotive industry. This form of registration can be used by about 20,000. Polish companies and research units. The project assumes that a document certifying that a vehicle, moped, agricultural tractor or trailer will be allowed to test driving will be a professional registration document together with professional registration plates. The professional registration will be completed by the senior and the authorized entity and will be valid 30 days from the date of completion, i.e. 6 months in the case of completion by the authorised body or test body of the manufacturer. It will also be possible to use one professional registration number for many vehicles. Professional registration card and professional registration plates will be able to use authorized entities which will gain from the old age the decision to register professionally. This will apply to entrepreneurs with their registered office or branch in Poland involved in the production, distribution or testing of vehicles, as well as to authorised entities, e.g. The Institute of Road Transport or the manufacturer's research units. The Starosta will issue a decision following submission of the draft documents and the payment of the administrative and registration fee. The fee for issuing a decision on professional registration of vehicles will not be higher than 100 PLN, and for a blank of professional registration - 20 PLN. Such registration will be valid for a year. The draft shall also specify the obligations of the rightholders, such as the need to keep a list of vehicles in service. He will also have to return old age professional registration documents and professional registration plates within 30 days of expiry or repealing the decision on professional registration of vehicles. The draft law also, of course, assumes the obligation to insure those vehicles which will use professional registration to prevent possible collisions that can always happen in traffic. The new rules also allow the collection of relevant data in the central vehicle register and allow for effective verification of the professional registration of vehicles by road control authorities. The test vehicles will be covered by compulsory insurance cover, OC insurance, as I already mentioned. The Act will enter into force 18 months after the date of the announcement. This deadline will allow you to issue a package of implementing rules and implement a system solution on the central vehicle registration side after launching the CEPiK 2.0 project . The bill was made available in the Public Information Bulletin on the website of the Government Legislative Centre in the service of government legislative works. No interested party reported any interest in the work on the project or comments. For this reason, we consider that it fulfils all the signs of a positive change of law, and therefore I ask the High House to accept the above-mentioned proposal. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u315	Thank you very much. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u316	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! Indeed, the Polish legal system lacks the solutions mentioned by the Minister, the lack of legal bases in this regard. This is very often a complaint from the public and those who would like to make just such professional registration of vehicles, because they are doing their business. We are meeting these expectations today. It is therefore necessary to reduce the administrative burden and to ensure that operators fulfilling certain conditions may use vehicles on a temporary basis using a professional registration card and one professional registration number for many vehicles without having to register the vehicle and visit the competent authority at any time. We're just saving time for entrepreneurs. In the current legal situation, it is the Law on Road Traffic, the governor shall, at the request of the owner of the vehicle and the authorised body, temporarily register each vehicle separately. This does not allow the use of a single registration number for many vehicles to perform test drives, because operators must register vehicles in old age each time. There is therefore a need to introduce a new form of temporary registration, that is professional registration of vehicles, which will simply improve this operation and allow authorized entities simply to save time, in short. This draft law proposes comprehensive new solutions for a new form of registration, starting with the submission by the entity of an application with the required documents for the decision on professional registration, and defines all the characteristics that are necessary to obtain this particular document. The possibility of professional registration according to the design will apply to motor vehicles, agricultural tractors, mopeds or trailers. They are defined in the relevant articles of the Road Traffic Law. The current provisions on the temporary registration of a vehicle do not give the above. I can't check it if I want to buy a car, I can't drive it on a public road. We're changing that by introducing a very good recipe. Now you can get in the car and know what you're buying. It is assumed that authorised entities, in particular vehicle distributors, will also be able to use professional vehicle registration not only for new vehicles but also for second-hand vehicles, including previously registered or imported from abroad. In the current legal state, indeed, a person interested in buying a vehicle, a potential buyer of a vehicle from an intermediary in the sale of an entity, is not able to carry out the driving personally, checking the vehicle. That's what we're changing. Vehicles imported from abroad include vehicles which are not registered in other Member States and in Poland, for example. agricultural tractors, trailers, mopeds. These vehicles are therefore imported without registration evidence and it is very necessary to introduce this particular form of short-term use of the vehicle in motion, called the professional registration of the vehicle in the project. This law, of course, affects the Law on Road Traffic, but also other laws. This is about the Code of Offences, the Road Transport Act and the Act of 22 May on compulsory insurance, Insurance Fund Guarantee and the Polish Office of Communication Insurers. All of this, of course, has been described precisely. Therefore, there is nothing left for me to recommend to the High House that further work be carried out on this very necessary and socially expected project. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u317	Thank you. Now on behalf of the Civic Platform Club - Mr Tomasz Kostus.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u318	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Minister! The proposed law amending the Law on Road Traffic and some other laws results from the need to regulate in the Polish legal system regulations allowing professional registration of vehicles, i.e. a new form of temporary registration. Such a procedure would significantly simplify the activities of operators involved in the distribution, production and research activities in which test vehicles are carried out. Currently, there is a lack of adequate legal bases in this area, which is a common charge formulated by Polish entrepreneurs, who, due to their activities, are interested in creating such a legal solution and consequently the possibility of using it effectively. As we read in the justification for the project, in the current legal state resulting from the Act of June 1997, the Law on Road Traffic is not possible for an operator operating in which, due to its specificities, many vehicles in road traffic may have to be used temporarily, using a single professional registration document for many vehicles. The lack of appropriate legislation is currently a major obstacle to the automotive industry and any activity in which there is a need for road tests. The current amendment is ultimately intended to introduce a solution that will allow the temporary exercise of the rights issued once for many cars without the need to register a vehicle each time and visit the registration body. This project provides for the possibility for operators fulfilling certain conditions of temporary use in road traffic of many vehicles using a professional registration document and assigned to the registration number document. The issue raised in the draft amendment concerns around 20,000. businesses and around 11 eligible entities, and the provisions proposed in this proposal are intended to regulate a significant, non-compliant issue and to provide an effective tool to reduce the administrative burden for the above mentioned entities. At the same time, this solution will serve the legitimate use of road vehicles with this form of registration while guaranteeing liability by the operators responsible in this regard. However, the solutions proposed by the legislator may lead to certain abuses in the use of professional registration documents by traders. Among the major threats are, among others. avoid the trader from issuing professional registration documents and limit this to the minimum necessary, e.g. A short time before the roadside inspection, as well as a situation in which the trader will not print out the registration evidence and will therefore not keep a list of vehicles in use. This creates some kind of difficulty, because the competent authorities will not be able to punish the driver for his offences, e.g. for speeding detected by a speed camera. Finally, since professional registration can be used to carry out a technical test, there are likely to be cases of use for vehicles which have not passed such tests e.g. due to road safety deficiencies. The mere assumption of professional registration shows that it may apply to vehicles which are still going to the test. Such a condition can continue without restrictions. Where registration documents are retained by authorised authorities, e.g. due to a road accident, the trader without any additional requirements will be able to issue another registration document. So much for doubt and danger. In addition to the penalties proposed by the authors in the framework of the current revision of the law, penalties should also be imposed for failure to return registration plates and non-used registration proof blanks, as well as other abuses that unfair traders may commit. In conclusion, it should be concluded that the proposed solutions [[♪ The bell ♪]] are beneficial for entrepreneurs and long-awaited by the automotive industry. Such legal solutions have been working successfully for many years in the West of Europe.

After possible adoption of the proposed legislation, in a certain way, there will be the possibility of temporary use in road traffic of many vehicles on the basis of a single registration document. The new procedure will simplify the activities of the entities involved in distribution, production or research, enabling professional registration to be used for testing vehicles within the framework of the activities carried out by those entities. This change is, I'd say, complex. The project envisages amending the Road Traffic Law Act, but also amending other laws to ensure the operation of the whole system. Ladies and gentlemen! I must admit, the solution proposed by the government is interesting. So far, there have been no rules that would allow professional entrepreneurs and other distribution, production or testing units to register vehicles in a way that is easier, faster than standard. Of course, the lack of legal bases in this area has already been the subject of intervention by entrepreneurs not only in the Ministry, but also - which is worth emphasising - in our parliamentary offices. According to estimates, as also mentioned by applicants, there are about 20,000 in Poland. Entrepreneurs and 11 eligible entities operate. As regards the details of the project, it should be noted that the professional registration will concern motor vehicles, agricultural tractors, mopeds or trailers as defined in Article respectively. 2 points 33, 44, 46 and 50 of the Road Traffic Law. As a result of the adoption of the proposed rules, it will be possible to perform test rides using professional registrations and professional license plates. Such registration shall be completed partly by the governor and partly by the authorized entity. This means that the operator carrying out the authorised activity will not have to register the vehicle at the office each time, but will receive one registration certificate and multiple-use plates which he will be able to mount on subsequent vehicles intended for testing or testing. Of course, such vehicles will have to meet the relevant technical requirements and the entrepreneur will have to provide the appropriate insurance. In summary, this solution will allow an authorised operator or entity to perform tests on different vehicles for the purpose of their business activity in the field of distribution, production or testing of vehicles. Importantly, the rating will apply not only to new vehicles but also to second-hand vehicles. The adoption of the project will also result in a reduction of administrative burdens. It's very important. However, in order not to be so colorful, I would like to conclude by mentioning some, I would say, shortcomings of the project. For example, the limitation of the temporary validity of professional registration or the limitation of the maximum number of blanks These are ideas we need to discuss. Unfortunately, in the next bill we see evidence of such, I would say, official phobias and expressions of extreme distrust of entrepreneurs. Minister, if we're making things easier, let's not complicate these facilitations. Let's trust the entrepreneurs. I hope that this project will not be another fight, and we will be able to discuss this substantively in committee. Therefore, on behalf of the Poselski Club I propose that the project be submitted to the Infrastructure Committee for further work. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

The President of the Republic of Poland Andrzej Duda in Katowice, in the seat of the Voivodeship office, signed under the metropolitan union law in the Śląskie voivodship. Once the requirements of the metropolitan area in the Śląskie voivodship are met by law, it should be established on 1 July 2017 and be operational on 1 January 2018. In view of the above, a discussion began on the name of the newly formed union, to which both the cities and municipalities located within the historical borders of Silesia and Zagłębie Dąbrowskie join. The local governments of Zagłębie Dąbrowski sent an appeal to the authorities of the Silesian Voivodeship to name the new union as the Silesian-Zęgłów Metropolia, arguing that, due to the identity of the region, the proposed name of the metropolitan union should take into account the interests of both Silesian and Zagłębian cities. Any omission of the name of the Dąbrowski Oblast in the name of the proposed metropolitan area or a reduction of its meaning, e.g. by adding the name Silesia, it is an attempt to forge the present reality. At this point it is noted that Zagłębie Dąbrowskie is a historical-geographical area of the present Silesian voivodship which includes in narrow terms, among others. cities such as Sosnowiec, Dąbrowa Górnicza, Będzin, Czeladź, Sławków, Wojkowice and in the broader view of the city and the communes of the Bydziński, Olkuski, Moszkowski and Zawiercieński districts, representing the north-eastern part of the GOP with a clearly crystallized local-cultural identity and long-term traditions in the field of metallurgy and mining. As a parliamentarian from the Dąbrowski Oblast, I fully support the initiative to name the newly created metropolis Metropolia Śląsko-Dąbrowska. I call on the authorities responsible for the shape and the name of the future metropolis to take into account the voice of the Dąbrowski Basin and, consequently, to support the proposed name. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u353	Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! Tomb guards are part of the Christian and national tradition. The custom of keeping worthy at Christ's symbolic graves is a living cultural monument with nearly 1000-year-old roots, which refers directly to the Golgotha Gospel. Branches of grave guards as formations for centuries created ad hoc during Easter were popular throughout Poland. Currently, as so-called. Turks survived mostly in Podkarpacie, where they create a unique mariage of Christian culture with folk customs. In recent decades in Podkarpacie we have observed an extraordinary Renaissance of the practice of grave guards. Nearly every municipality hosts local parades of this Easter army. They are an opportunity to present various costumes, elements of the parade drill and meetings with similar formations from other localities. For organizers it is not only a promotion, but also a form of distinction. Such events on the voivodship scale have been organized in various villages of Podkarpacie for 25 years, and for 14 years in the national rank. Two weeks after Easter, there are huge crowds of viewers from the region, the country, and even from far corners of Europe. High Izbo! The inventory and protection of any sign of intangible cultural heritage is our national and patriotic duty. For several years Poland has been a signatory of the UNESCO Convention, which even imposes such an obligation on us. In the Podkarpackie region, the tradition of graveyard guards has passed from generation to generation for hundreds of years, and it is common for a father and his grandson to parade in the branch. For young people it is a great honor to stand guard at Christ's symbolic grave. Guards are an important element of the cultural identity of Podkarpacie, a distinguishing feature on the map of the country and Europe, a brick in building local patriotism. Therefore, the church and local authorities of the municipality of Grodzisko Dolne apply for the National Heritage Institute to include the rite of grave guards on the national list of intangible cultural heritage. I think that we must all help to achieve this goal. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-05-24-sejm-42-1.u361	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On 14 November 2016, the Puławy City Council adopted a resolution on the creation of the Czartoryski Museum by the votes of the Councillors of Law and Justice. Thanks to the support of the Ministry of Culture, Czartoryski Foundations, city authorities, and my own actions, the museum in the city was able to reactivate, which was the first in Poland to gather collections of great value for our national heritage. It was Princess Isabella Czartoryska who opened the famous Sybil Temple in 1801, creating the first Polish museum with a collection of national souvenirs. The opening of the Czartoryski Museum in Puławy took place on 14 May 2017. The honorable guest of the event was Duchess Dr. Barbara Czartoryska. The opening was attended, among others. representatives of local authorities, managers of the most important cultural and educational institutions in the region, representatives of the Czartoryski Museum in Cracow, members of the Czartoryski family, directors of scientific institutes, and of course speaking these words. The concert of Alice Węgorzewska and Robert Grud has been inaugurated. The audience heard the songs of Fryderyk Chopin, as well as the music of Baroque and Romanticism. The music was accompanied by fragments of poetry by Karol Wojtyla, Adam Mickiewicz, John Paul Woronicz and Maria Konopnicka performed by Artur Kalicki, a television journalist who led the concert. During this event, a permanent exhibition entitled "The Bible's Word" was opened. Motherland, let me immortalize you. The frames were completed by the 18th-century fashion show performed by the historical reconstruction group of the Stanisławowski Society. The ceremony was accompanied by a presentation of the first one purchased by the Czartoryski Museum in Puławy - a letter by Adam Kazimierz Czartoryski to Anna Hiżowa from Puławy in 1811. At the end of the ceremony there was a march to the temple of Sybil with a symbolic key made especially for this occasion. I pay my respects to the organizers and all those who contributed to the reactivation of the Czartoryski Museum in Puławy. I would like to express my special thanks to the Councillors of the Law and Justice of Puławy, who with their determination, stubbornness and consequence only supported the idea of creating a museum with their own voices, so that we could finally rejoice in its opening. The director of the museum Honorat Mielniczenenko and all Puławy residents wish it to become a glory and a card of the city. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

The powerful blow given to the Catholic majority of the Zielona Góra community by the fact that its religious freedoms and practices were reduced, the collection of the Catholic House resulted in mass protest of the population. It is estimated that in less than 40 thousand inhabitants of Zielona Góra, about 5 thousand were arrested on the streets, 300 were arrested, nearly 200 were sentenced. Repressions and oppression lasted for years. At the head of the protest was the parish priest Fr. Kazimierz Michalski, former soldier, prisoner of concentration camps, who showed integrity also to Communists and their temptations to eliminate the influence of the Church in communist Poland. He became a hero fighting for God and honor. He's been followed by the Green Gorges. Therefore, if I speak of this today from the Seymary Court, for two reasons - respect and respect for people who stood up for the freedom of religion, the Church and the community, but also because I hope that the extraction from the memory of the historical city of this heroic break of its inhabitants will result in a new good, even if by building consciously the identity of the city and its inhabitants on the heroics of the previous events of May 1960, even if by reconnecting with a life-giving cycle between the early years of Polish presence in Western lands, with their charism and cultural hospitality of people with borders and dedication of Poland and contemporary times. Giving the city its distinctive features, special, passionate and beautiful, so that the Green Mountain is not nothing, and certainly not red. [[Applause]] [[Voice from the room: Bravo!]]

Henryk Dobrzański-Hubala in Sokółka held a meeting of farmers and young people. During the Mass the medals of St. Izidora Oracza for the merits of spreading the cult the patron saint of farmers. For years the co-organizers have been the Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Development, the Marshal's Office of Podlaskie Voivodeship, Podlaskie Voivodeship Office and many other local organizations. The main initiator is the Association of Saint Izydor Orach. It is a great honor and honor for me, because I have been associated almost always with the tradition of the folk movement in Podlasie, and St. Izidor Orach is not only the patron saint of farmers, but also my personal signpost. Like this great saint, I was raised by my parents in faithfulness to the maxim: pray and work, and God will help you. This is the maxim I use in my life until today. The Association of Saint Izydor Orach is a comprehensive promotion of his cult, it is a concern for the development of our agriculture, it is the recognition of people who have been associated with work in the countryside for years. During all these years, Białystok bishops and priests have been very supportive of us. Fr. Archbishop Stanisław Szymecki, metropolitan Białystok Edward Ozorowski, Fr. Prelate Józef Wiśniewski, Fr. Canon dean Andrzej Gniedziejko, pastor of farmers, and many self-governments, lecturers, farmers and people of good heart. Thanks to this support, it will certainly be easier for us to achieve the objectives set, knowing that agriculture in the Podlaskie province still plays a big role and is always the beginning of the discussion about rural and agricultural matters. Let us remember the patronage of Izidora Oraczu and his message: pray and work, and God will help you.
